# ema-cashub, standing order 42 (2026-11-15): the no-breakage census of Cargo's 20 px section gap below 1025 px (gate-so42-cargo-section-gap.txt) on every served Cargo copy, as you asked for the documents it reaches without a recording.
# Method: each of the 990 published Cargo paths (order 41's list) loaded on cargo.turnsthe.page at 390 x 844 and at 1000 x 900, unhinted, CONSENT=1; measured as served, then the rule added as a style sheet and measured again in the same load (the in-page toggle: both sides from one load). lh-proxy, www.* but www.mynewsdesk.com and every airline host refused and counted: 28 requests refused at each width (24 and 4 in two of the five batches, the pages' widget calls), none sent.
# Per width: the page's height and the footer's top (as served -> with the rule), then with the rule: the scroll width, elements running past the right edge (carousel tracks and aria-hidden copies not counted), sections overlapping the one before, sections with a negative margin; "BROKEN" when any of those four grows.
# Result: 0 of 990 broken at either width (scroll width 390 / 1000 everywhere, as served too). 390: 939 pages shorter by 24 to 196 px, 51 unchanged, none taller. 1000: 929 shorter by up to 196 px, 56 unchanged; on 5 sustainable-development-goals pages (de, es, fr, it, pt) the scroll height reads +14 to +55 px while the footer moves up 28 px as on the others: something outside the sections (not drawn in the sections' flow) changed between the two reads; nothing overlaps or runs past the edge there. 910 of the 939 pages that move at 390 have no recording.
